CAS starts from an identified interventional coalition game, allocates the joint intervention contrast with causal Shapley contributions, and converts those raw outcome-scale effects into Local CAS, Signed Local CAS, and two complementary Global CAS summaries. The innovation is not a new Shapley formula, but a local-to-global causal reporting layer with an explicit intervention target. In the known-truth benchmark, eight repeated primary-interaction simulations (n = 2,200 each, three actions) gave mean Local CAS MAE of 0.107 for coalition-aware CAS, compared with 0.173 for one-at-a-time normalisation and 0.213 for a global normalised absolute ATE vector. The paired advantage over one-at-a-time normalisation increased from -0.003 under additivity to 0.091 under strong interactions. On both empirical DoubleML datasets, 401(k) eligibility/net financial assets (n = 9,915) and Pennsylvania reemployment bonus/unemployment duration (n = 5,099), predictive SHAP/TreeSHAP rankings differed materially from Feature-CAS rankings of treatment-effect modifiers. In Pennsylvania, dep1 (exactly one dependent) moved from predictive global rank 13 to Feature-CAS rank 2 and was the leading local Feature-CAS modifier. These results isolate the added value of separating what predicts the outcome from what explains heterogeneity in an estimated causal effect.

</details>


### [181] [Trie Automata for Constrained Decoding over Large Finite Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12574)
*Xingzi Xu,Karim Bouyarmane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"trie automaton"的专用约束解码机制，通过利用有限集合的结构特性（共享前缀、有界深度、已知基数），使用Aho-Corasick多模式匹配预计算每个节点的token掩码，从而绕过通用语法编译的"基数墙"瓶颈，在vLLM批处理场景下实现29倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型需要生成符合预定义模式的约束输出，其中常见约束是从有限有效字符串集合中选择。当前的约束解码系统依赖通用语法编译，当有效值数量增长到数千时变得极其缓慢，形成"基数墙"问题。

Method: 提出了trie automaton机制，利用有限集合的结构特性（共享前缀、有界深度、已知基数），通过Aho-Corasick多模式匹配预计算每个节点的token掩码。预计算掩码实现了无状态的服务路径，绕过了引导解码管道。

Result: 与XGrammar相比，每步有效token计算快7倍（0.65us vs 5.8us），K>=300时编译快2-6.5倍。端到端vLLM吞吐量在batch size=256时达到219 req/s vs XGrammar的7.5 req/s（29倍）。在7种tokenizer系列（32K-262K词表）中，K=10,000时编译保持在100ms以下，每步成本与集合大小无关，且保证100%输出有效性。

Conclusion: trie automaton通过利用有限集合的固有结构特性，成功解决了通用语法编译在规模增长时的性能瓶颈，实现了数量级的性能提升，同时保持100%的输出有效性保证。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly need to generate structured outputs that conform to predefined schemas, with one common constraint being selection from a finite set of valid strings. Current constrained decoding systems handle this through general-purpose grammar compilation, which becomes prohibitively slow as the number of valid values grows into the thousands, a cardinality wall. We introduce the trie automaton, a specialized mechanism that exploits finite-set structure (shared prefixes, bounded depth, known cardinality) via Aho-Corasick multi-pattern matching to precompute per-node token masks. The trie achieves 7X faster per-step valid-token computation (0.65 us vs. 5.8 us) compared to XGrammar, one of the primary backends in vLLM and SGLang, and 2--6.5X faster compilation at K >= 300. Because precomputed masks enable a stateless serving path that bypasses the guided decoding pipeline, this advantage compounds in batch serving: end-to-end vLLM throughput reaches 219 req/s vs. XGrammar's 7.5 req/s at batch size 256 (29X). The 29X combines the algorithmic speedup with integration-path savings that only precomputed masks can unlock. Across seven tokenizer families (32K--262K vocabulary), the trie maintains sub-100ms compilation up to K = 10,000 and flat per-step cost regardless of set size, while guaranteeing 100% output validity.

</details>


### [182] [Reasoning Jury: Multi-Model Consensus for Evaluating Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12585)
*Congchao Wang,Diwakar Singh,Qiaozi Gao,Spyros Matsoukas,Yang Liu,Mahdi Namazifar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Reasoning Jury系统，用一组开源LLM组成的"陪审团"替代单一模型来评判推理过程的缺陷，通过主持人引导的讨论和共识机制，在推理缺陷识别上超越前沿闭源模型，同时成本仅为后者的8-15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理LLM需要高质量的推理痕迹评判能力，但单一模型（即使是最前沿的模型）在长推理痕迹上识别缺陷的效果不佳；同时，在线训练中使用前沿模型受到使用条款限制。

Method: 提出Reasoning Jury系统，用多个开源LLM组成陪审团，并采用主持人引导的审议机制——陪审员之间相互批评彼此的判断并可修改初始投票，最终由主持人通过审议或判断整合达成共识。

Result: 开源模型陪审团（如gpt-oss-120b）在识别推理缺陷方面显著优于前沿闭源模型（opus-4.6、sonnet-4.6、gemini-3.1-pro），且总成本仅为前沿模型的8-15%。

Conclusion: 陪审团机制能提升推理缺陷识别质量，同时降低成本；这些判断还可用于深入理解推理LLM在基准测试上的失败模式。

Abstract: Improving reasoning LLMs requires the ability to judge the quality of long reasoning traces for effective reasoning data curation, strong training signals during reinforcement learning, and an in-depth understanding of reasoning behaviors during model performance evaluation. Additionally, surfacing reasoning mistakes that the model makes would enable improving the model's performance at runtime through providing feedback. Due to the difficulty of this complex task on long reasoning traces, single-model judges (even frontier models) do not do well at identifying reasoning defects. Additionally, leveraging frontier models during online training of reasoning LLMs is generally prohibited due to guardrails in terms of use. In this work, we introduce Reasoning Jury, a system that replaces the single judge with a jury of LLMs and a moderated consensus mechanism, to improve the fidelity of judgments for identifying reasoning defects. In reasoning jury, defects of a reasoning trace and their severity are surfaced through a deliberation where a moderator conducts a discussion amongst the jury where the jurors critique each other's judgments and get to modify their initial votes. The moderator derives a consensus through deliberation amongst jurors or consolidation of judgements. We show that Reasoning Jury with a jury of open-weight models (e.g., gpt-oss-120b) is able to significantly outperform frontier models (opus-4.6, sonnet-4.6, and gemini-3.1-pro) at correctly identifying reasoning defects. Besides accuracy performance improvements, the aggregated cost of the jury (initial verdicts, deliberations, consolidation, etc.) is a fraction (8 to 15%) of the cost of running frontier models in LLM-as-a-judge setup. We also show how these judgements can be leveraged to understand failure modes of reasoning LLMs on benchmarks, which allows much deeper understanding of a model's performance.

</details>


### [183] [Auditable agentic AI for evidence-grounded thyroid ultrasound diagnosis and reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12590)
*Haifan Gong,Shiyu Chen,Bodong Wang,Yuqi Wang,Shijie Wang,Guoliang You,Xinyu Xiong,Haowei Wang,Mingzhi Mao,Dexing Kong,Qinghua Liu,Wei Lou,Fei Chen,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ThyroidXAgent，一个临床医生可交互的智能体AI系统，用于甲状腺超声诊断与报告生成。系统整合了结节分割、定位、测量、风险评估和报告生成等任务，输出可审计的病例级证据记录，经多中心大规模数据验证，显著提升了诊断准确性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲状腺超声诊断需要协调病灶定位、测量、风险分层和报告撰写等多个步骤，但现有AI系统往往孤立地解决单个任务，缺乏对临床审查流程的支持，导致诊断效率低、可审计性差。

Method: 开发了ThyroidXAgent智能体系统，协调多个专业诊断工具并存储输出为可审计的病例级证据记录；构建了OpenThyroidDB多中心多任务数据集（约30万张超声图像和2.4万份配对报告），并引入ThyClinScore语义评估指标对报告质量进行评价。

Result: 在28,458例非重叠测试数据上，结节分割平均Dice为87.21%，良恶性分类平均AUROC为0.9466；淋巴结转移预测和滤泡vs乳头状癌分类AUROC分别为0.864和0.805；报告诊断一致性从70.3%提升至86.2%，分割和报告时间分别减少35.9%和27.4%。

Conclusion: 证据支持的甲状腺超声诊断智能体系统具备可审计性、临床医生可纠错性，能显著提升诊断准确性和工作效率，为临床决策支持提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Thyroid ultrasound diagnosis requires coordinated lesion localization, measurement, risk stratification and reporting, yet most AI systems address these tasks in isolation and provide limited support for clinical review. We present ThyroidXAgent, a clinician-interactive agentic AI system that coordinates specialized diagnostic tools and stores their outputs as an auditable case-level evidence record. The system was developed using OpenThyroidDB, a multicentre, multitask resource integrating approximately 0.3 million ultrasound images and 24,000 paired reports, and was evaluated on 28,458 non-overlapping test cases, including 8,721 cases from 35 centres in the private NHC-MISD-TUS cohort. Across heterogeneous datasets, ThyroidXAgent achieved a mean Dice score of 87.21 percent for nodule segmentation and a mean AUROC of 0.9466 for benign-malignant classification. The same workflow supported lymph-node metastasis prediction and follicular versus papillary thyroid carcinoma classification, with AUROCs of 0.864 and 0.805, respectively. For report generation, evidence-grounded assembly outperformed multimodal language-model baselines across three cohorts. ThyClinScore, a lesion-level clinical semantic metric introduced here, showed the strongest correlation with a location-aware language-model judge. ThyroidXAgent improved physician classification accuracy, increased report diagnostic consistency from 70.3 percent to 86.2 percent, and reduced segmentation and reporting time by 35.9 percent and 27.4 percent, respectively. These findings support auditable, clinician-correctable agentic AI for thyroid ultrasound diagnosis and reporting.

</details>


### [184] [DiG-bench: Discovery in Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12593)
*Ruairidh M. Battleday,Kai Sandbrink,Jimi Cullen-Drohan,Zihan Yan,Timothy Muller,Clare Maguire,Ales Kubicek,Fraser Greenlee-Scott,Sukrit Sumant,Tri Dao,Jürgen Schmidhuber,Michal Valko,Joshua Tenenbaum,Thomas L. Griffiths,Zeb Kurth-Nelson,James C. R. Whittington*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DiG-bench（Discovery in Games）基准，包含70个独立游戏，用于评估AI代理在未知目标条件下通过交互实验发现新知识的能力。游戏分为七个难度等级，其中21个公开，其余保密用于安全评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现是科学过程的核心部分，但现有AI基准很少直接测试在未知目标条件下通过实验发现新知识的能力，存在评估空白。

Method: 构建DiG-bench基准，包含70个独立游戏，每个游戏编码为短字符串，具有需要通过交互和实验发现的独特变换规则。游戏设置多个关卡验证规则是否被发现，且关卡的通关条件也是未知的。游戏分为七个难度等级，21个公开测试，其余保密。

Result: 最低难度等级可被多个模型常规解决，而最高难度等级对最优秀的代理框架模型也具有挑战性。所有70个游戏均被至少一名人类首次尝试即成功解决。

Conclusion: DiG-bench填补了AI发现能力基准的空白，提供了一个可控环境下评估AI新知识发现能力的标准测试平台，同时通过保密子集确保评估的安全性。

Abstract: Discovery---formulating novel generalizations---is a central part of the scientific process. Despite its importance, there is a gap in the current AI benchmark landscape, with few benchmarks directly probing the capacity for discovering new knowledge with experimentation in controlled environments where the objective is unknown. To address this gap, we release a new benchmark: DiG-bench (Discovery in Games). DiG-bench consists of a set of 70 independent games. Each game is encoded as a short string and has unique transformation rules that must be discovered through interaction and experimentation. The levels of the game present a series of challenges to test whether the rules have been discovered, where the win conditions for each level are also unknown. We provide games at seven tiers of difficulty for AI agents. The lowest tier is routinely solvable by multiple models, while the highest tier challenges the best models in agentic harnesses. All 70 games were solved by at least one human on first attempt. A subset of 21 games is released publicly, and the remainder is held private for secure evaluation.

</details>


### [185] [Dead text or binding clause? Measuring and restoring constraint influence in black-box LLM dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12599)
*Haoyuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了多轮对话中用户撤回约束（revocation）后模型仍持续执行已撤销要求的问题，称为"行为复发"或"撤销惯性"。系统通过合约账本（contract ledger）、顺序消融探针（sequential ablation probe）和修复阶梯（repair ladder）三个组件，仅通过模型API即可实现按条款测量、提前预测和预算内修复。实验表明提前编译可显著降低复发率，探针能提前预测复发（AUROC良好），且开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮对话中用户可以随时撤销约束，但模型经常继续执行已撤销的要求，甚至出现在注释声称已移除的情况下仍执行的现象。现有方法无法按条款测量这种失效、无法在交付前预测、也无法在匹配预算下修复。

Method: 提出SysName系统，包含三个组件：(1)契约账本——为每个约束配对一个可执行检查器，将撤销记录为墓碑（tombstone），并提前将净约束状态编译为单个规范；(2)顺序消融探针——测量每条条款的遵从度和增量行为影响；(3)修复阶梯——在token和尝试次数匹配的预算下进行修复。

Result: 在HumanEval任务（含NClauses个验证检查器）上，8B模型的复发率随约束负载从ScaleDelayedMTwo升至ScaleDelayedMEight，而更强模型处于最低水平。提前编译显著降低复发率（与无账本的验证器重试基线相比），自适应阶梯干预无额外增益。探针预测复发的AUROC为主要指标，单句墓碑提示可恢复约三分之一的编译效果。

Conclusion: 撤销失败从一种不可见的状态属性变为可测量、可预测、可修复的对话状态属性，交付开销仅CostDeliveryFactor倍，每个结果的总API计算成本为CostTotalHedged。

Abstract: Multi-turn dialogues let users revoke constraints as easily as impose them, but revocation does not reliably take effect: models keep enacting withdrawn requirements (occasionally beneath comments asserting their removal), a failure we call \emph{behavioral relapse}, or revocation inertia. No existing instrument measures this influence per clause, predicts it before delivery, or repairs it under matched budgets. \sysname{} closes the three gaps through the model API alone: a contract ledger pairs every constraint with an executable checker, records revocations as tombstones, and compiles the net constraint state ahead of time into a single specification; a sequential ablation probe measures per-clause adherence and incremental behavioral effect; a repair ladder operates under token- and attempt-matched budgets. On \dataname{} (\NTasks{} HumanEval tasks, \NClauses{} verified checkers), relapse at an 8B operating point climbs from \ScaleDelayedMTwo{} to \ScaleDelayedMEight{} as constraint load grows, while stronger models sit at floor. Under matched checkers, model, and budget, ahead-of-time compilation significantly reduces relapse against a no-ledger verifier-retry baseline (\RestoreDiff{}, 95\% CI \RestoreDiffCI{}, $p$ \RestoreDiffP{}); adaptive ladder interventions stacked on top add no detectable gain (95\% confidence excludes gains $\geq$ \LadderExcludedGain{}). The probe predicts relapse before delivery (AUROC \AurocPrimary{}); a one-sentence tombstone note recovers about a third of the compilation effect and survives a placebo control. At \CostDeliveryFactor{} delivery overhead and \CostTotalHedged{} of API compute for every result, revocation failure becomes a measurable, predictable, and repairable property of dialogue state rather than an invisible one.

</details>


### [186] [@skills: Attention is all you have](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12610)
*Li Yin,Zhi Li,Zhan Shi,Haoran Zhang,Haebin Seong,Zhangyang,Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出@skills协议，将技能安装解耦为内容、持久化和自动触发三个功能，仅自动触发需要提示词驻留，通过路径寻址实现"无需安装即可使用"的技能交付方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前有56,804个公开的代理技能，但主导的交付方式（安装）导致技能描述竞争有限的系统提示词槽位（不到100个），长尾技能没有实际使用路径。安装将三个可分离的功能捆绑在一起，造成资源浪费和使用障碍。

Method: 提出@skills开放协议，将安装的三个功能（内容、持久化、自动触发）解耦。通过路径寻址任何技能、子树或集合，读取即使用无需安装；vendor操作可将技能拷贝到项目Git树中；仅自动触发需要一行.gitignore风格配置驻留提示词；目录即菜单；无需manifest、lockfile或注册。

Result: @skills以可安装包形式分发，通过单一指令文件将任何能读文件、运行命令的代理变为客户端。配套提供免费hub（atskills.one）用于语料库搜索、排序、私有/团队集合及一站式创作，但hub可选，gh:和本地路径无需hub即可解析。

Conclusion: 该协议将技能交付从"少装多用"转向"少装多用"（Install less, use more），解决系统提示词稀缺空间与技能长尾之间的矛盾，为代理技能生态提供更高效的发现与使用机制。

Abstract: There are 56,804 public agent skills today, and teams write many more privately. The dominant delivery model is installation: once installed, a skill's description remains in the system prompt, competing for fewer than 100 reliable trigger slots. This leaves the long tail with no practical path to use and forces teams' own playbooks to compete for the same scarce space. We observe that installation bundles three separable functions: content, persistence, and automatic triggering. Only the last requires prompt residency. We therefore propose @skills, an open protocol that separates them. A path addresses any skill, subtree, or collection, and reading a skill is sufficient to use it, so nothing is installed or made resident. The operation vendors a copy at the same path into a project's Git-tracked tree for adaptation and ownership. The operation adds one .gitignore-style line, the only element that costs prompt residency. A directory is a menu, making bundles ordinary directories rather than all-or-nothing units. The protocol requires no manifest, lockfile, or registration, and SKILL.md remains unchanged. @skills is additive, ships as an installable package, and turns any agent that can read files and run commands into a client through a single instruction file. Its open specification is at https://github.com/SylphAI-Inc/atskills and it is implemented in the AdaL CLI at https://adalagent.ai . Because paths address skills well but cannot find them, the protocol is paired with a free hub at https://atskills.one for corpus-wide search and ranking, repository-free hosting, private and team collections, and one-screen authoring. The hub is optional: gh: and local paths resolve without it, and indexed GitHub skills retain their gh: identities. Install less, use more.

</details>


### [187] [Jagged Judges: Epistemic Stability Under Silence, Pressure, and Persistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12645)
*Justin Zhao,Himaghna Bhattacharjee,Hannah Korevaar,Bhaktipriya Radharapu,Khalid El-Arini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Wiggle Framework，一个用于测试LLM裁判（LLM Judge）认知稳定性的统一压力测试框架，从机械一致性、单轮信念和多轮坚持三个维度评估裁判的稳定性，并发现所有前沿模型在遭受挑战或压力时都会明显改变判决结果，且这种改变大多是背离真实答案的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM裁判系统被广泛用于模型评估、在线评分和奖励建模，但现有的准确性验证无法反映裁判在重新提示、质疑或持续施压下的稳定性，缺乏统一的压力测试框架来评估裁判的认知鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Wiggle Framework，从三个维度分解裁判的鲁棒性：机械一致性（重新提示和重构框架下的稳定性）、单轮信念（单次挑战下的稳定性）和多轮坚持（持续或自适应压力下的稳定性）。利用该框架对9个前沿模型在14个评判任务上进行了系统测试。

Result: 所有模型在作为裁判时都表现出显著的"摇摆"：在静态施压下25-71%的情况下翻转判决，在使用对抗性LLM说服者时62-91%的情况下翻转。研究发现能成功改变裁判判决的压力几乎总是对真实答案是有害的。基线陪审团多数强度被识别为预测哪些项目会摇摆的最有效单信号。

Conclusion: 这是第一个在裁判场景下对机械一致性、从众性和可说服性测试进行跨数据集苹果对苹果比较的研究，表明LLM裁判的认知稳定性存在系统性问题，需要更多关注。

Abstract: LLM judges have become central infrastructure for model evaluations, online grading, and reward modeling. Judges are typically validated by accuracy on golden data, but accuracy says little about whether they are stable under re-prompting, challenge, or sustained pushback. We introduce the \emph{Wiggle Framework}, a unified stress test for epistemic stability in LLM judges. The framework decomposes judge robustness along three dimensions: Mechanical Consistency (stability under re-prompting and reframing), Single-turn Conviction (stability under a single challenge), and Multi-turn Persistence (stability under sustained or adaptive pressure). We use the framework to study 9 frontier models across 14 judging tasks spanning safety, toxicity, AI writing detection, and political-response evaluation. Every model exhibits substantial wiggle as a judge --- flipping verdicts 25--71\% of the time under static pushback, and 62--91\% with an adversarial LLM persuader. Critically, we find that pressure that succeeds in changing a judge's verdict is almost always net-corrupting with respect to ground truth. Beyond the framework itself, we identify baseline jury majority strength as the most effective single-shot signal for anticipating which items wiggle. Taken together, this is the first apples-to-apples cross-dataset comparison of mechanical, conformity, and persuadability tests in a judging context.

</details>


### [188] [SteerBench-Work: A Benchmark for Agent Steering at Action Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12654)
*Oguz Serdar,Cuneyt Mertayak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SteerBench-Work，一个以真实事件为锚点、双向设计的基准测试，用于评估LLM代理在工作场景中"越过门槛"（proceed）与"暂停审查"（hold）的决策校准能力。涵盖开发运维、客服、金融、法律、医疗、HR和安全等7个领域，包含106个场景及证据反转镜像和校准控制。研究发现：模型错误方向高度不对称（过度拒绝28.1% vs 错误放行1.0%），且通用能力与"门槛校准"并非同一回事。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时运行的LLM代理通过工具执行操作，每一步都可能产生重大后果（发邮件、合并PR、转账付款）。关键的决策节点在于"提交前选择"——是继续执行还是暂停等待人工或策略审查。现有基准缺乏对这一特定边界决策的系统评估，因此需要专门针对工作场景代理的门控决策基准。

Method: 构建SteerBench-Work基准：(1) 以公开事件为锚点设计106个场景，覆盖7个领域；(2) 每个场景配备证据反转的镜像版本（mirror），用于测试模型对证据的敏感性；(3) 包含校准控制组（calibration controls）；(4) 标签在"继续"和"暂停"间近乎均衡分布，使两类错误获得相近的测试机会；(5) 模型接收提议的操作和可用证据，输出门控决策并被评分。评估了30种模型条件。

Result: 30种模型条件下的错误高度不对称：模型在28.1%的机会中错误地暂停了已获授权且证据清晰的工作，而错误放行不安全操作仅占1.0%。最难的案例是"风险已解析的提交"（风险已由签名或结构化证据清除的场景）。模型在著名事件的证据反转镜像上得分（63.8%）显著低于原始事件（98.5%）。更高能力的模型往往在提交边界上过度拒绝。

Conclusion: 通用能力不等于门槛校准：更强的模型可能过度拒绝，更多的推理可以修复薄弱门控但不会提升校准良好的门控。这说明LLM代理的安全部署需要专门的校准评估与优化，而非仅依赖通用能力的提升。

Abstract: Long-running LLM agents act through tools, and a single step can send an email, merge a pull request, or wire a payment. The steering decision is the pre-commit choice at that boundary: proceed, or hold for human or policy review. We introduce SteerBench-Work, an incident-anchored, bidirectional benchmark for that decision in workplace agents across developer operations, customer service, finance, legal, medical, HR, and security.
  Release v2026-05 contains 106 scenarios anchored in public incidents, paired evidence-reversed mirrors, and calibration controls, with labels split nearly evenly between proceed and hold so the two error directions get near-identical numbers of chances. A model sees the proposed action and the available evidence, returns a gate decision, and is scored on whether it crosses or holds the boundary correctly. Across 30 model conditions the failures run almost entirely in one direction: models wrongly hold authorized, evidence-cleared work on 28.1% of opportunities and wrongly allow unsafe work on 1.0%. The hardest cases are risk-resolved commits, where signed or structured evidence has already cleared a real risk trigger, and models score markedly worse on evidence-reversed mirrors of famous incidents (63.8%) than on the incidents themselves (98.5%). General capability is not the same as steering calibration: higher-capability models often over-refuse at the commit boundary, and more reasoning can repair a weak gate while leaving a calibrated one flat. The public leaderboard is at steerbench.com.

</details>


### [189] [General Probabilities of Causation with Causal Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12657)
*Xin Shu,Zhen Lei,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了多值因果概率（PoCs）的边界收紧问题，通过引入协变量和中介变量中的因果信息，推导出比现有非二元边界更紧的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已将概率因果（PoCs）从二元设置扩展到多值设置并推导出理论边界，但尚不清楚额外的因果知识能否进一步收紧多值设置下的边界。

Method: 在Tian和Pearl以及Li和Pearl、Shu等人工作的基础上，通过将协变量和中介变量中包含的因果信息编码到多值PoCs的边界推导中，得到更紧的边界。

Result: 通过玩具示例说明理论结果，并通过模拟研究证明所提出的边界比现有非二元边界更紧。

Conclusion: 协变量和中介变量中的因果信息可以有效收紧多值设置下概率因果的识别边界，提升参数识别的精度。

Abstract: Probabilities of causation (PoCs) characterize individual causal responses that cannot be directly observed and therefore generally require partial identification. Tian and Pearl first derived theoretically sharp bounds for binary PoCs, including the probability of necessity (PN), the probability of sufficiency (PS), and the probability of necessity and sufficiency (PNS). Mueller et al. subsequently tightened the bounds for binary PNS by incorporating causal information encoded in covariates and mediators. More recently, Li and Pearl, as well as Shu et al., extended PoCs to multivalued settings and derived corresponding theoretical bounds. These developments naturally raise the question of whether additional causal knowledge can further tighten the bounds in multivalued settings. This paper addresses this question by deriving tighter bounds for multivalued PoCs through the incorporation of causal information encoded in covariates and mediators. We illustrate the theoretical results with toy examples, while simulation studies further demonstrate that the proposed bounds are tighter than existing nonbinary bounds.

</details>


### [190] [Designing AI Pipelines for Decision-Ready ITSM Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12670)
*Archan Dutta,Yash Dharmadhikari,Marat Valiullin,Rahul Guha,Alexander Liss*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种社会技术AI管道，将IT服务管理（ITSM）系统的异构工单数据转化为面向高管的决策支持工具，结合LLM模式规范化、HDBSCAN子主题聚类和层次凝聚聚类，生成多层次的决策支持产物。通过设计科学研究方法进行评估，结果显示可解释性、可操作性、信任度和使用可能性四项指标均超过4.0/5.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ITSM系统积累了大量异构工单数据，但销售和高管等利益相关者难以将其转化为可操作的决策情报，存在数据到洞察的转化鸿沟。

Method: 采用设计科学研究原则，构建了结合LLM模式规范化、HDBSCAN子主题聚类和层次凝聚聚类的AI管道，将原始ITSM导出数据转化为多层级的决策支持产物（主主题和子主题）。

Result: 六个产物和五位来自销售工程与客户成功角色的评估者参与利益相关者评估，四项决策支持指标（可解释性、可操作性、信任度和使用可能性）平均均超过4.0/5.0，其中信任度是最一致的信号。

Conclusion: 将ITSM分析定位为信息系统（IS）层面的问题，涉及数据转化、抽象化和以人为中心的设计，为ITSM数据驱动决策提供了可验证的框架。

Abstract: IT service management (ITSM) systems accumulate large volumes of heterogeneous ticket data that are difficult for sales and executive stakeholders to convert into actionable intelligence. This paper presents a sociotechnical AI pipeline, designed and evaluated following design science research principles, that transforms raw ITSM exports into a multilevel decision-support artifact. The pipeline combines LLM-based schema normalization, HDBSCAN sub-topic clustering, and hierarchical agglomerative clustering to generate executive-facing Main-topics and granular Sub-topics. A stakeholder evaluation across six artifacts and five raters from Sales Engineering and customer success roles shows that all four decision-support metrics, interpretability, actionability, trust, and likelihood of use, on average exceed 4.0 out of 5.0, with trust as the most consistent signal. The findings position ITSM analytics as an Information Systems (IS) problem of transformation, abstraction, and human-centered design.

</details>


### [191] [On the Expressive Power of Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12671)
*Phokion Kolaitis,Rik Sengupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是基于电路复杂性的视角，系统综述了多层Transformer作为语言识别器的表达能力研究成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层Transformer是现代LLM的核心组件，但其精确的表达能力尚未完全明确。作者希望通过电路复杂性理论来精确刻画Transformer的表达能力，将其与理论计算机科学中经典的计算模型进行比较。

Method: 作者将Transformer按资源（如注意力、精度）进行参数化，并与不同类型、规模和深度的电路类进行比较，从而在电路复杂性框架下分析Transformer的表达能力，并综述了该领域的关键研究成果。

Result: 电路复杂性已被证明是分析Transformer表达能力最合适的理论分支，文中综述了多项将Transformer与不同电路类进行精确对应的研究成果。

Conclusion: 通过电路复杂性方法，可以系统且精确地刻画Transformer的表达能力，为该领域未来的研究奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Multi-layer transformers form the critical component of essentially all large language models (LLMs) in use today. Because of their ubiquity and computational capability, there is a rapidly growing body of work that aims to precisely calibrate the expressive power of transformers as language recognizers by comparing them against standard models of computation studied for decades by the theoretical computer science community. In this endeavor, circuit complexity has by and large emerged as the "correct" branch of computational complexity to analyze the expressive power of transformers; the reason is that parameterizing transformers by the various resources they use, such as attention and precision, leads to direct comparisons with different classes of circuits parameterized by resources such as type of gates, size, and depth. Here, we present an overview of selected results that delineate the expressive power of transformers using concepts and methods from circuit complexity.

</details>


### [192] [Lines and Ladders: A Context-Aware Multi-Agent Framework for Large-Scale Retail Price Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12674)
*Ravi Teja Chunduri,Srikaran Reddy Boya,Deep Narayan Mishra,Ajay Kumar B,Karthik Kumaran,Pranay Kona*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于多智能体LLM框架的自动化系统，用于构建零售业"Lines and Ladders"定价分类体系，在真实企业数据上取得了优于单智能体基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球零售商需要维护价格一致性并执行每日低价策略，但数百万个活跃商品的目录使得人工管理价格关系不可行。不一致的商品变体定价会扭曲客户价值感知并蚕食销售额。

Method: 提出了一个可扩展的、上下文感知的多智能体LLM框架，通过专门的LLM智能体来自动化构建"Lines and Ladders"定价分类体系。该框架通过识别关键属性、提取多模态价值、应用分层分组逻辑来构建连贯的定价结构。系统采用3个智能体的设计。

Result: 在真实企业数据上，3-Agent系统在Lines上取得0.83的F1分数，优于单智能体基线（通过缓解认知过载）。在食品与消费品类目实现>90%精确率和>75%召回率，在非结构化的一般商品目录中实现80.2%的分配准确率，并已在生产环境部署。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM框架能够有效自动化构建零售定价分类体系，通过分工缓解单智能体的认知过载，在真实生产环境中表现优异。

Abstract: Maintaining price consistency and executing an Every Day Low Price strategy is critical for global retailers. However, with catalogs spanning millions of active items, manual governance of price relationships is infeasible. Inconsistent pricing across item variants distorts customer value perception and cannibalizes sales. To address this, we present a scalable, context-aware Multi-Agent Framework designed to automate the construction of "Lines and Ladders" pricing taxonomies. Our framework employs specialized LLM agents to construct these coherent pricing structures by identifying key attributes, extracting multi-modal values, and applying hierarchical grouping logic. Evaluated on real-world enterprise data and deployed in production, our 3-Agent system achieves an F1-score of 0.83 for Lines, outperforming single-agent baselines by mitigating cognitive overload. The system achieves >90% precision and >75% recall in Food & Consumables, and 80.2% assignment accuracy in the unstructured General Merchandise catalog.

</details>


### [193] [Privacy-Preserving RAG by Concealing Sensitive Information from External LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12675)
*Saleh Almohaimeed,Saad Almohaimeed,Mousa Jari,Fahad Alotaibi,Khalid A. Alobaid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SEAG（敏感实体别名生成器），一个隐私保护框架，通过在将查询和检索文档发送给外部生成器之前，定位并替换其中的敏感实体为别名，从而使用户能够在保护隐私的同时利用强大的第三方大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG隐私研究主要关注防止未授权用户访问敏感数据，但忽略了外部生成器可以访问包含机密信息的查询和检索文档这一隐私风险。用户在使用第三方生成器时可能泄露敏感信息。

Method: SEAG框架包含一个轻量级模型，负责定位敏感实体、生成对应别名，并构建实体替换表。该表用于在将用户查询和检索文档转发给外部生成器之前替换其中的敏感词汇。论文构建了两个数据集：一个用于微调SEAG模型生成实体替换表，另一个用于评估整个框架。

Result: 实验表明SEAG框架表现成功。在User指标（衡量模型向用户提供正确回复同时对外部生成器隐藏敏感信息的能力）上，所有SEAG模型均达到80%以上的准确率。SEAG模型Qwen-3、LLaMA-3.2和Phi-4在隐藏所有敏感实体方面的总准确率分别为77.83%、76.73%和74.91%。

Conclusion: SEAG框架为解决RAG场景中外部生成器访问敏感信息的隐私问题提供了有效方案，在不牺牲回答质量的前提下保护用户隐私。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is widely used to improve the performance of Large Language Models (LLMs) in answering user queries. Existing privacy research on RAG has focused on preventing unauthorized users from accessing sensitive data. However, another important problem that is often overlooked in RAG privacy research is that external generators have access to the query and the retrieved documents, which may contain confidential information that could potentially be misused or accessed for unintended purposes. In this paper, we introduce the Sensitive Entity Alias Generator (SEAG), a privacy-preserving framework that empowers users to utilize powerful third-party generators without disclosing sensitive information. SEAG introduces a lightweight model that locates sensitive entities, generates corresponding aliases, and constructs an entity replacement table. The table is used to replace sensitive words in the user's query and in the retrieved documents before they are forwarded to an external generator. For this purpose, two datasets were constructed: one for fine-tuning SEAG models to generate entity replacement tables, and another for evaluating the entire SEAG framework. The experimental results demonstrate the success of the SEAG framework. As for the User metric, which measures the ability of the model to provide a correct response to the user while hiding sensitive information from the external generator, all SEAG models achieved over 80% accuracy. Additional analysis further evaluated the ability of SEAG models Qwen-3, LLaMA-3.2, and Phi-4 to hide all sensitive entities within given documents. The results show good performance with total accuracies of 77.83%, 76.73%, and 74.91%, respectively.

</details>


### [194] [The Role of Natural Language Understanding in Multimodal Video-Based Dengue Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12677)
*Danial Sharifrazi,Saadat Behzadi,Julakha Jahan Jui,Mojtaba Mohammadi,Nouman Javed,Roohallah Alizadehsani,Prasad N. Paradkar,Asim Bhatti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于YOLO和CLIP的视觉-语言框架，用于从视频数据中分类未感染和感染登革热病毒2型（DENV2）的蚊子飞行帧，在帧级别达到98.54%的准确率和99.91%的灵敏度，时间聚合后实现完全的视频级别分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视频数据中检测蚊子感染相关的行为变化具有挑战性，因为蚊子体积小、运动快速且不规则，同时受背景、光照和阴影等环境因素影响，导致可靠的特征提取困难。

Method: 首先使用YOLO从背景中分离蚊子区域，然后提取视频帧的视觉特征，与生物学上有意义的文本提示在共享嵌入空间中对齐。多模态模型通过监督双向对比学习进行微调，并基于帧级图像-文本相似度进行分类。

Result: 帧级别达到98.54%准确率和99.91%灵敏度，时间聚合后实现完全的视频级别分类性能。消融实验表明微调和CLIP表示至关重要，文本分支提供语义图像-文本对齐但未比纯视觉模型带来精度优势。

Conclusion: 视觉-语言模型可为分析视频数据中感染相关的生物行为提供有用框架。

Abstract: Detecting infection-related behavioral changes in mosquitoes from video data is challenging because mosquitoes are small, move rapidly and irregularly, and are affected by environmental factors such as background, lighting, and shadows, which can make reliable feature extraction difficult. In this study, a YOLO- and Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP)-based vision-language framework is proposed to classify mosquito flight frames of uninfected and Dengue virus serotype 2 (DENV2)-infected mosquitoes. First, YOLO is used to isolate mosquito regions from the background. Then, visual features extracted from video frames are aligned with biologically meaningful textual prompts in a shared embedding space. The multimodal model was fine-tuned using supervised bidirectional contrastive learning and evaluated through frame-level image-text similarity-based classification. The results show that the proposed method achieved 98.54% accuracy and 99.91% sensitivity at the frame level. After temporal aggregation of frame-level information, the model achieved complete video-level performance. The ablation results showed that fine-tuning and CLIP-based representations were essential for this domain, while the textual branch provided semantic image-text alignment rather than an accuracy advantage over the vision-only model. These findings suggest that vision-language models can provide a useful framework for analyzing infection-related biological behaviors from video data.

</details>


### [195] [Beyond the Best Guess: Improving LLM Solution Coverage with Evolution Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12679)
*Conor F. Hayes,Elliot Meyerson,Kajetan Schweighofer,Roberto Dailey,Babak Hodjat,Risto Miikkulainen,Xin Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用进化策略（ES）替代强化学习（RL）对大型语言模型进行后训练，以提升pass@k多样性和解覆盖率，在数学和科学等发现型任务中取得更好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在发现型领域（如数学、科学）中，常用的pass@k方法需要通过多样化解探索来提升效果，但标准RL后训练会导致输出分布收窄、解覆盖塌缩，限制了pass@k性能。

Method: 采用进化策略（ES），一种基于种群、无梯度的后训练方法，直接在权重空间通过随机扰动进行优化，相较于RL能够保持更宽的输出分布和更强的解覆盖能力。

Result: ES相比RL持续获得更高的pass@k分数，产生更广的输出分布和更好的解覆盖，在标准数学基准测试上取得更优结果。

Conclusion: ES为发现型问题及其他需要多样化解覆盖的关键领域提供了更好的后训练基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in discovery domains such as math and science. The usual approach is to present the problem to the model and use its answer as the proposed solution. However, beyond this best guess, discovery can be enhanced by increasing test-time compute. In a process called pass@k, the model is allowed to explore the solution space and generate diverse candidate solutions. Unfortunately, the standard approach to post-training LLMs through Reinforcement Learning (RL) may limit pass@k: the model's output distribution narrows around high-reward outputs, causing the solution coverage to collapse. The alternative is to use Evolution Strategies (ES), a population-based, gradient-free post-training method that optimizes directly in weight space through random perturbations. As this paper shows, ES achieves consistently higher pass@k than RL and produces a broader output distribution with greater solution coverage. This coverage in turn makes it possible to achieve better results in e.g. standard math benchmarks. Thus, ES provides a better foundation for post-training in discovery problems and other domains where diverse solution coverage is critical.

</details>


### [196] [Spatial Memory Agent: Experience-Grounded Procedure Memory for Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12743)
*Haokai Zhang,Yuhang Ding,Yunshu Zhou,Xinze Du,Shengtao Zhang,Zhiyue Zhao,Yuling Xi,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出空间记忆代理（SMA），一种无需参数更新的运行时框架，通过将可验证的空间经验转化为可复用的迁移性经验教训，使冻结的VLM代理在推理时无需外部专家工具即可自我进化并提升空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提升VLM空间推理能力的方法主要依赖后训练（如SFT和RL）或调用外部空间工具，但冻结模型在推理时无法依赖外部专家工具的场景下如何自我进化尚未被充分探索。

Method: SMA包含三个核心组件：1）验证器引导的反思机制，将空间经验蒸馏为紧凑可迁移的经验教训；2）迁移可靠性评分（TRS），通过后续检索结果校准每个教训的未来迁移可靠性；3）只读部署阶段，通过语义过滤和相似度-TRS联合排序检索经验教训，引导冻结模型推理。

Result: 在5个空间基准和4个基础VLM上，SMA在每个基础模型组中均取得最高宏平均成绩，在20个评估中大部分取得最佳准确率。

Conclusion: SMA建立了实用的参数更新免费的空间自我进化路径，适用于不同规模的冻结模型和环境，为无需外部工具的VLM空间推理增强提供了新范式。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence is becoming a foundation for embodied agents, robotic planning, and multimodal assistants. To improve the spatial reasoning ability of VLM agents, existing work has mainly followed two lines. One line uses post-training methods, such as supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Another line adopts an agentic paradigm in which the model calls external spatial tools, such as depth estimation and 3D reconstruction tools, to gather intermediate spatial evidence. We study a complementary and underexplored route: Can a frozen VLM agent improve its spatial reasoning through \textbf{parameter-update-free self-evolution}, without depending on external expert spatial tools at inference time? We present \textbf{Spatial Memory Agent (SMA)}, an \textbf{experience-grounded runtime framework} that converts verified spatial experience into reusable transferable lessons. In a verifiable spatial environment, SMA queries the frozen VLM, obtains a predicted answer and reward, and uses \textbf{verifier-guided reflection} to distill compact transferable lessons from spatial experience. SMA further assigns each lesson a \textbf{Transfer Reliability Score (TRS)}, which is initialized uniformly and calibrated from later retrieval outcomes as visit evidence of future transfer reliability. During \textbf{read-only deployment}, SMA retrieves lessons by semantic filter and similarity-TRS combined ranking, allowing the retrieved memory to guide frozen model inference. Across five representative spatial benchmarks and four base VLMs, SMA achieves the highest macro average in every base-model block and the best accuracy among the evaluated methods in most of the 20 evaluations, establishing a practical parameter-update-free path for spatial self-evolution across the evaluated frozen model scales and environments.

</details>


### [197] [Correct Is Not Governed: Provenance Integrity in Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12761)
*Jesus Salas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Matrix，一个确定性因果状态层，通过记录权威与事实依赖、验证完成证据、选择性失效受影响工作，使智能体工作流具备可审计性和独立可验证性，适用于机构场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体工作流仅以最终结果正确性作为评估标准在机构场景中是不充分的，因为正确行为可能依赖错误的权威、缺乏支持的完成声明，或被后续变更变得过时。

Method: 提出"受治理执行"概念，并构建Matrix，一个确定性因果状态层，记录权威与事实依赖、验证完成证据、选择性失效受影响的工作。

Result: 在受控对比中，受治理与直接工作流常达到相同结果，但只有受治理路径持续保留治理证据、拒绝无支持的关闭、并将恢复限制在依赖任务中。然而，在角色分离的迁移挑战中，确定性强制完整性契约过度阻止了在其创作上下文之外产生的合成数据包。

Conclusion: Matrix并非通用准确性增强器，其核心定位是作为机构完整性层，使智能体工作可审计且可独立验证。

Abstract: Agentic workflows are commonly evaluated by whether they reach the correct outcome. That is insufficient in institutional settings, where a correct action may rely on the wrong authority, an unsupported completion claim, or work made stale by a later change. We define governed execution as work whose decisions, completion, and response to change are supported by inspectable provenance. We present Matrix, a deterministic causal-state layer that records authority and fact dependencies, verifies completion evidence, and selectively invalidates affected work. Across controlled comparisons, governed and direct workflows often reached the same outcomes, but only the governed path consistently preserved governing evidence, refused unsupported closure, and limited recovery to dependent tasks. A role-separated transfer challenge then failed: a deterministically enforced completeness contract severely over-blocked synthetic packets produced outside its authoring context. These results do not establish Matrix as a general accuracy enhancer; they support its primary role as an institutional integrity layer for making agentic work auditable and independently verifiable.

</details>


### [198] [PROVE-RT: Generating Mechanized Theorem Prover Scripts for Real-Time Systems using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12762)
*Sadat Shahriyar,Shareef Ahmed,Abdullah Al Arafat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PROVE-RT框架，利用大语言模型辅助生成PROSA/ROCQ机械化定理证明脚本，用于实时系统可调度性分析的形式化验证，在评测集上达到44.7%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实时系统可调度性分析测试依赖手工证明，难以扩展和验证；机械化验证（PROSA/ROCQ）虽然严谨但需要大量领域专业知识和证明工程工作。虽然LLM在多种任务上表现优异，但缺乏PROSA特定知识，无法直接生成正确的机械化证明脚本。

Method: PROVE-RT通过多种策略引导LLM生成PROSA/ROCQ脚本：依赖感知的非正式草图生成、检索处理过的PROSA文档、分阶段骨架生成以及证明补全。同时构建了一个面向机械化的语料库，包含来自1,191篇实时系统论文的13,134个带依赖信息的非正式草图。

Result: 在精选的评测集上，直接提示SOTA LLM无法可靠生成有效的PROSA机械化证明，而PROVE-RT达到了44.7%的成功率。

Conclusion: 检索引导和分阶段的LLM辅助方法能够有效提升可调度性分析在PROSA/ROCQ中的自动化机械化水平。

Abstract: Schedulability analysis is essential for certifying real-time systems, but existing tests are often developed through pen-and-paper proofs that are difficult to scale, validate, and maintain. Mechanized verification in PROSA/ROCQ offers a rigorous alternative, yet manually constructing such proofs requires substantial domain expertise and proof-engineering effort. Recent successes of large language models (LLMs) across a wide range of tasks make them promising candidates for generating PROSA/ROCQ scripts for mechanized theorem provers. However, state-of-the-art LLMs often lack the PROSA-specific knowledge required to correctly use its modeling abstractions and proof patterns.
  This paper introduces PROVE-RT, an LLM-assisted framework for generating PROSA/ROCQ scripts to mechanize schedulability analyses in real-time systems literature. PROVE-RT guides generation through dependency-aware informal sketches, retrieval from processed PROSA documentation, staged skeleton generation, and proof completion. We construct a mechanization-oriented corpus from 1, 191 real-time systems papers, containing 13, 134 informal sketches with dependency information. On a curated evaluation set, direct prompting of state-of-the-art LLMs fails to reliably generate valid PROSA mechanizations, whereas PROVE-RT achieves a success rate of 44.7%. These results show that retrieval-guided and staged LLM assistance can improve automated mechanization of schedulability analysis in PROSA/ROCQ.

</details>


### [199] [ARAC: Benchmarking Auto-Research's Alignment and Completeness on End-to-End Researchs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12788)
*Jiale Cui,Yueyao Yuan,Kaixi Zhong,Xiaogang Xu,Jiafei Wu,Zhe Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ARAC-Bench，一个用于评估Auto-Research系统研究轨迹与人类研究行为对齐度和完整性的评测框架，通过学术认知技能系统和三阶段能力诊断协议，对11个SOTA框架进行评估，最佳对齐分数仅为67.9/100，与博士候选人排名相关性达0.8141。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Auto-Research快速发展带来了根本性的评测挑战：如何衡量其研究轨迹与人类研究行为在对齐度、逻辑连贯性和演化完整性上的一致性。现有评测方法仅关注最终答案匹配，无法评估研究过程的质量。

Method: 提出ARAC-Bench框架，包含两个协同组件：(1)学术认知技能系统——首次将隐性审稿人专业知识转化为分阶段校准的可量化评分标准；(2)三阶段能力诊断协议——在严格模块化约束下将研究过程分解为提案、实验、综合三个可追踪且相互独立的维度。

Result: 对11个SOTA框架的系统评估显示最佳对齐分数仅为67.9/100，揭示模拟严谨人类方法论存在显著差距；与博士候选人排名的验证显示强相关性0.8141，确认ARAC-Bench能够可靠反映研究者真正重视的维度。

Conclusion: ARAC-Bench不仅提供细粒度的诊断工具，还可作为可扩展的奖励信号用于训练下一代自主研究系统。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Auto-Research has surfaced a fundamental evaluation challenge: how can we measure the alignment, logical coherence, and evolutionary completeness of its research trajectory with human research behavior? We propose Auto-Research's Alignment and Completeness, ARAC-Bench: a Researcher-Mimicking Evaluation framework that shifts the objective from matching final answers to reproducing high-quality human research processes. The framework operates through two synergistic components: the Academic Cognition Skills system, which is the first to transforms implicit reviewer expertise into stage-calibrated, quantifiable rubrics; and a three-stage capability diagnostic protocol, which decomposes the research process under strict modular constraints into three traceable, mutually independent dimensions: Proposal, Experiment, and Synthesis. Systematic evaluation of 11 SOTA frameworks yields a best alignment score of only 67.9 of 100, revealing a significant gap in simulating rigorous human methodology. Validation against Ph.D. Candidates rankings shows a strong correlation of 0.8141, confirming that ARAC-Bench reliably reflects the dimensions researchers truly value. ARAC-Bench provides not only a fine-grained diagnostic tool but also a scalable reward signal for training the next generation of autonomous research systems.

</details>


### [200] [CABS+: Efficient and Scalable Model Merging via Conflict-Aware Sparsification and Adaptive Weight Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12842)
*Yuchen Liu,Zongzhen Yang,Binhang Qi,Hailong Sun,Xiang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CABS+，是对模型合并方法CABS的扩展。CABS+通过自适应权重分配（AWA）的梯度-free搜索替代网格搜索以降低时间复杂度，并引入非对称适应度函数提升多任务整体性能。同时提出相对协同分数（RSS）量化模型可合并性并指导模型选择。在27个数据集和5种模型上验证，相比AdaMerging和WUDIMerging分别提升16.97%和12.93%的性能，GPU内存仅为AdaMerging的25%，合并速度比WUDIMerging快近4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并作为构建多任务模型的范式受到关注，但存在参数冲突和知识干扰问题。已有方法CABS通过结构化剪枝和顺序掩码减少干扰，但其依赖网格搜索导致指数级时间复杂度的不足，且优化目标可能被高性能任务主导，导致整体性能次优。

Method: 提出了CABS+方法，包含两个核心创新：1）自适应权重分配（AWA），采用梯度-free搜索优化合并系数，降低时间复杂度；2）非对称适应度函数，促进各任务间更均衡的性能提升。此外提出相对协同分数（RSS）用于量化模型可合并性并指导模型选择。

Result: 在27个数据集和5种模型（涵盖大语言模型、小规模语言模型和视觉模型）上进行了实验。相比AdaMerging提升16.97%整体性能，相比WUDIMerging提升12.93%；在不同任务数和模型架构下表现更稳定和鲁棒；GPU内存使用仅为AdaMerging的25%以下；合并速度相比WUDIMerging提升近4倍。

Conclusion: CABS+通过自适应权重分配和非对称适应度函数有效解决了CABS的高时间复杂度和优化目标失衡问题，同时在性能、稳定性、内存效率和速度方面均显著优于现有方法，并提供了RSS指标为模型合并选择提供指导。

Abstract: Model merging has recently attracted significant attention as a promising paradigm for constructing unified multi-task models without requiring additional retraining. However, parameter conflicts and knowledge interference across tasks often degrade merged-model performance. Prior work introduced Conflict-Aware and Balanced Sparsification (CABS), which reduces parameter interference through structured pruning and sequential masking. However, CABS relies on grid search to determine scaling coefficients, resulting in exponential time complexity, while its optimization objective can be dominated by high-performance tasks, leading to suboptimal overall performance. To address these limitations, we extend CABS and propose CABS+. Specifically, Adaptive Weight Allocation (AWA) optimizes merging coefficients via a gradient-free search scheme to reduce time complexity, while an asymmetric fitness function promotes more comprehensive performance gains across tasks. Moreover, we conduct a systematic empirical study of key factors influencing model merging performance and propose Relative Synergy Score (RSS) to quantify model mergeability and guide model selection. We compare CABS+ with state-of-the-art model merging methods, including CABS, AdaMerging, and WUDIMerging, across 27 datasets and 5 models covering large language, small-scale language, and vision models. Extensive experiments verify the effectiveness and efficiency of CABS+. Compared with AdaMerging and WUDIMerging, CABS+ improves overall performance by 16.97% and 12.93%, respectively, exhibits stronger stability and robustness across varying task numbers and model architectures, uses less than 25% of the GPU memory required by AdaMerging, and achieves nearly a 4x speedup in merging time over WUDIMerging.

</details>


### [201] [Beyond Retrieval: Query-Conditioned Reuse of Long-Horizon Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12847)
*Yifei Li,Heng Wang,Lingling Zhang,Muye Huang,Xinyu Zhang,Jiashuai Liu,Hang Yan,Rongman Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"检索后复用"（post-retrieval reuse）是长时程轨迹记忆的独立瓶颈，并设计了一个固定检索、目标状态、模型、解码和工具预算的评估框架，通过查询条件复用（QCR）提供目标绑定的支持信息，在WebArena、WorkArena和AppWorld的2,391个目标实例上取得了62.3%的平均成功率，比完整轨迹注入高10.7个百分点，同时节省48.9%的在线token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索系统能够找到相关的历史轨迹，但在用户、实体、约束或环境状态发生变化后，智能体并不知道如何复用这些轨迹。作者认为这一"检索后复用"步骤是长时程轨迹记忆中的一个被忽视的瓶颈。

Method: 提出一个评估框架，固定候选检索、目标状态、模型、解码和工具预算，仅改变提供给智能体的支持信息。在此基础上提出查询条件复用（QCR），即一种简单的目标绑定笔记格式，记录可复用过程、需恢复的绑定、适用条件和验证要求，用于检验复用假设。

Result: 在2,391个目标实例上，QCR达到62.3%平均成功率，比完整轨迹注入高10.7个百分点，且节省48.9%的在线token。摘要重排序能以94.8%的概率选出可复用记忆，端到端成功率距oracle选择器仅1.8个百分点。分析表明轨迹越长或源-目标绑定变化越大，直接轨迹注入的效用损失越严重。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将检索质量与"将检索到的经验转化为对新任务安全有用的支持"这一挑战分离开来，证明目标绑定的复用支持比原始轨迹注入更有效、更高效。

Abstract: Retrieval can identify a past trajectory that may matter, yet it does not specify how an acting agent should use that trajectory after users, entities, constraints, or environment state have changed. We identify this post-retrieval reuse step as a distinct bottleneck for long-horizon trajectory memory and formulate an evaluation framework that holds candidate retrieval, target state, model, decoding, and tool budget fixed while varying the support delivered to the agent. We instantiate the framework with query-conditioned reuse (QCR), a deliberately simple target-bound note that records a reusable procedure, bindings to recover, applicability conditions, and verification requirements. QCR serves to test the reuse hypothesis rather than to claim a universally preferred memory format. Across 2,391 target instances in WebArena, WorkArena, and AppWorld, QCR reaches 62.3% average Success, 10.7 points above Full Trajectory, while using 48.9% fewer online tokens. Summary reranking selects a reusable memory for 94.8% of targets, placing end-task Success within 1.8 points of an oracle reusable selector. Analyses by trajectory length and source--target binding shift show that direct trajectory injection loses much of its utility as traces grow longer or source-specific values change, whereas target-bound support preserves a larger share of the measured gain. The resulting framework separates retrieval quality from the problem of turning retrieved experience into safe, useful support for a new task.

</details>


### [202] [Practice Makes Unsafe: Skill Misevolution in Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12851)
*Xutao Mao,Liangjie Zhao,Xiang Zheng,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillMisevo-Gym和SkillMisevo-Bench，用于评测LLM智能体在技能演化过程中因不安全经验被持久化为可复用策略而导致的安全风险，并引入SafeEvolve包装器来修复不安全内容并治理后续复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体自我改进时会将有价值的轨迹转化为可复用的跨任务状态（技能），但一个不安全的成功轨迹在触发输入消失后可能被固化为可复用策略。技能演化优化的是任务结果而非过程安全，因此被污染的经验可能导致技能错误演化（misevolution）。现有基准只能评测当前行为或静态产物，无法归因贯穿编写、检索和执行全生命周期的风险。

Method: 作者提出了SkillMisevo-Gym（一个生命周期感知的测试平台，跨智能体框架对技能状态进行版本管理）和SkillMisevo-Bench（从恶意暴露到后续任务的冻结设计，包含概念对齐的良性任务和九个生命周期指标），并引入SafeEvolve包装器用于修复不安全内容并治理后续复用。实验覆盖25种智能体-方法配置，每种配置涵盖525个任务、25个回合。

Result: 所有21种演化配置都会生成不安全产物，但只有15种会导致新会话中的实际危害。在暴露扫描中，3个恶意任务将跨任务ASR从16.0%提升至35.3%。SafeEvolve将不安全检索和新会话危害分别降低26.7和17.3个百分点，而良性效用的平均变化仅为0.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 持久化适应安全性必须同时治理"更新写入了什么"和"未来执行者复用了什么"这两个环节。代码已开源。

Abstract: Self-improving LLM agents convert successful trajectories into persistent cross-task state. An unsafe success can thereby become reusable policy after its triggering input disappears. Skill evolution makes this failure measurable by distilling operational trajectories into executable, transferable, and inspectable procedures. Because evolution optimizes task outcomes rather than procedure safety, compromised experience can cause skill misevolution. Existing benchmarks measure current behavior or static artifacts but cannot attribute risk across authoring, retrieval, and later execution. To expose this lifecycle, we introduce SkillMisevo-Gym, a lifecycle-aware harness that versions skill state across agent frameworks, and SkillMisevo-Bench, a frozen design from malicious exposure to carryover tasks, with concept-aligned benign tasks and nine lifecycle metrics. We also introduce SafeEvolve, a wrapper that repairs unsafe content and governs subsequent reuse. Across 25 agent-method configurations, each covering 525 tasks in 25 episodes, all 21 evolved configurations author unsafe artifacts, while only fifteen lead to fresh-session harm. In the exposure sweep, three malicious tasks raise carryover ASR from 16.0% to 35.3%. Across representative skill evolution methods, SafeEvolve reduces unsafe retrieval and fresh-session harm by 26.7 and 17.3 percentage points, respectively, while mean benign utility changes by only 0.4 points. Together, persistent-adaptation safety must govern what updates write and what future executors reuse. Code is available at https://github.com/henrymao2004/misevolve.

</details>


### [203] [AI and Consumer Rights in India Working Paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12863)
*Omir Kumar,Sriya Sridhar,Vibhav Mithal,Balaraman Ravindran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨印度《消费者保护法（2019）》是否足以应对AI产品和服务造成的损害，以及该法案能否在AI价值链中合理分配责任。研究发现法案的宽泛定义可能适用于AI相关事件，但在因果关系证明、责任主体划分等方面存在显著缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统在面向消费者的应用中日渐普及，AI相关损害的归责问题尚未解决，需要审视现有法律框架是否充分应对。

Method: 对印度《消费者保护法》（2019年）的产品责任、损害及缺陷定义进行法律文本分析，并结合AI价值链的复杂特性评估其适用性与不足。

Result: 该法案的技术中立定义可能适用于AI相关事故（如人身伤害、心理伤害、偏见输出及失控等），但存在两大关键缺口：一是证明AI缺陷与消费者损害之间的因果关系面临技术性挑战；二是该法案假设制造商、销售商、服务提供者角色分明，而AI价值链中数据提供者、模型开发者、部署者与用户的职责重叠，无法简单映射。

Conclusion: 现有的责任框架缺乏处理复杂多利益相关方AI损害的比例机制，虽然法案可能涵盖AI实体，但在执法层面仍需厘清特定行业的监管重叠问题。

Abstract: As AI systems proliferate in consumer facing applications, questions about liability for AI related harms remain unresolved. This working paper examines whether India's Consumer Protection Act, 2019, adequately addresses harm caused by defective AI products and services, and whether it proportionately allocates liability across the AI value chain.
  The Act's broad definitions of product liability, harm, and deficiency appear technology agnostic and potentially applicable to AI related incidents including personal injury, psychological harm, biased outputs, and loss of control. However, significant gaps remain. Proving causation between AI defects and consumer harm presents a technical challenge, as AI failures often stem from design choices rather than discrete defects. Additionally, the Act's framework assumes distinct roles for manufacturers, sellers, and service providers, yet the AI value chain involves overlapping responsibilities among data providers, model developers, deployers, and users that do not neatly map to these categories. Current liability frameworks lack proportionate mechanisms to effectively address complex, multistakeholder AI harms. While the Act may cover AI entities, enforcement requires clarification on sector specific overlaps.

</details>


### [204] [ReflectFact: Self-Reflective Agents for Improving Comprehension and Reasoning in Multi-Hop Fact Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12877)
*Runze Zhao,Zixin Tang,Xiaoshuai Hao,Leyuan Chang,Xiaopeng Fu,Boyu Qiao,Dongyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReflectFact，一个用于多跳事实验证的自反思智能体框架，通过显式推理路径规划、证据漂移验证和推理反思验证三个关键任务，解决了现有方法中智能体推理偏离全局目标以及参数知识与证据冲突的问题，在HOVER和EX-FEVER数据集上均取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多智能体协作的多跳事实验证方法存在两个关键局限：(1) 智能体在执行子任务时缺乏对全局验证目标的认知，导致推理偏离预期方向；(2) 参数知识与提供的证据之间的冲突会削弱基于证据的推理并导致错误判定。这些问题严重影响了多跳事实验证的准确性。

Method: ReflectFact框架包含三个关键任务：(1) 显式推理路径规划——通过解析隐含实体、将声明分解为子问题、整合已验证事实构建基于证据的推理路径；(2) 证据漂移验证——当基于证据的答案仅反映参数先验时，强制智能体引用支持证据重新作答，校准证据偏差；(3) 推理反思验证——从全局任务视角重新检查每个推理步骤，检测到不一致时重新生成，纠正位置偏差和替换偏差等推理缺陷。最终聚合验证过的推理链以产生可靠判定。

Result: 在HOVER和EX-FEVER两个数据集上的大量实验表明，ReflectFact有效弥补了现有方法的理解和推理缺陷，取得了最优性能，在两个数据集上分别比最强基线高出3.32%和2.78%。

Conclusion: ReflectFact通过自反思机制有效解决了多智能体协作在多跳事实验证中的推理偏移和证据偏差问题，验证了自反思框架在事实核查领域的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-hop fact verification, which verifies claims by reasoning over multiple pieces of evidence, is critical for combating misinformation on social media yet remains highly challenging. Recent methods primarily rely on multi-agent collaboration to decompose fact verification into specialized subtasks. However, these methods face two critical limitations: (1) agents may perform individual subtasks without sufficient awareness of the global verification objective, causing their reasoning to deviate from the intended direction; and (2) conflicts between parametric knowledge and the provided evidence may undermine evidence-grounded reasoning and lead to incorrect verdicts. To address these challenges, we propose ReflectFact, a novel self-reflective agent framework for multi-hop fact verification. ReflectFact introduces three key tasks. Explicit Reasoning Path Planning builds an evidence-grounded reasoning path by resolving implicit entities, decomposing the claim into sub-questions, and integrating the verified facts into a verdict. Evidence-Drift Verification makes the agent re-answer by quoting the supporting evidence when a grounded answer merely echoes its parametric prior, thereby calibrating evidence deviation to ensure grounded comprehension. Reasoning Reflection Verification re-examines each reasoning step and regenerates it once an inconsistency is detected, correcting reasoning flaws such as location bias and replacement bias through a global task perspective. Subsequently, the agent aggregates validated reasoning chains to yield reliable verdicts. Extensive experiments on HOVER and EX-FEVER demonstrate that ReflectFact effectively remedies the comprehension and reasoning defects of existing methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance and respectively outperforming the strongest baseline by 3.32\% and 2.78\% on the two datasets.

</details>


### [205] [Predictive Memory Localization: Forecasting Selective Intervention Paths from Internal Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12892)
*Jinhao Jing,Tian Zeyu,Lucas Qingyang Fang,Zhisheng Chen,Shuang Chen,Yuhao Luo,Qiannian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出预测性记忆定位（PML）方法，将测量网格干预路径作为记忆定位的预测对象，分离随机校准的目标移动与语义邻居和能力的损伤，通过强度分离的低剂量因果响应来比较静态定位和监督几何。在9个数据集、14个领域的3000条记录上评估了30,000条路径和210,000种强度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导将局部化表示转化为控制方向，但仅靠局部化本身无法揭示方向是否具有选择性操作区间。需要一种方法来判断方向干预的选择性效果。

Method: PML将测量网格干预路径作为记忆定位的预测对象，分离随机校准目标移动与语义邻居/能力损伤，采用强度分离的低剂量因果响应比较静态定位与监督几何，并通过预测驱动选择器在密集扫描中决定系数或放弃干预。

Result: 在层7，几何推导的RFM/AGOP方向达到13.1%目标任意和12.3%清洁任意，超过随机3.6和3.4个百分点。|α|=0.1处的响应是|α|∈{0.25,0.5}不连续强度结果的最强信号。预测驱动选择器相比固定强度策略降低了语义邻居损伤，低剂量响应在三个基础模型上达到0.801-0.828的AUROC。

Conclusion: PML将记忆定位转化为边际级别选择性结果的可验证预测和风险感知的干预决策工具。

Abstract: Activation steering turns localized representations into control directions, but localization alone does not reveal whether a direction has a selective operating regime. We introduce Predictive Memory Localization (PML), which treats the measured-grid intervention path as the predictive object of memory localization. PML separates random-calibrated target movement from semantic-neighbor and capability damage, and compares static localization and supervised geometry with a strength-disjoint low-dose causal response. Our frozen study covers 3,000 records from nine datasets and fourteen domains, yielding 30,000 distinct record-direction-layer paths and 210,000 distinct path-strength evaluations. At layer 7, the geometry-derived RFM/AGOP direction reaches 13.1% target-any and 12.3% clean-any, exceeding random by 3.6 and 3.4 percentage points under a record-paired bootstrap. Across record-, dataset-, and domain-grouped splits, responses at $|α|=0.1$ are the strongest signal for outcomes at disjoint strengths $|α|\in\{0.25,0.5\}$. On held-out records, a predictor-driven selector chooses a coefficient or abstains, improves utility and reduces semantic-neighbor damage relative to a train-tuned fixed-strength policy, and avoids most evaluations in a dense scan. Across three residual-norm-matched base models, learned directions retain selective-path gains and low-dose responses yield 0.801-0.828 record-held-out macro AUROC. PML therefore turns memory localization into a falsifiable forecast of margin-level selective outcomes and a risk-aware intervention decision.

</details>


### [206] [Polish Medical Visual Question Answering: Vision-Language Models Underutilize Visual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12928)
*Jakub Pokrywka,Łukasz Grzybowski,Antoni Lasik,Marek Kubis,Jeremi Ignacy Kaczmarek,Wojciech Kusa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个波兰语医学视觉问答（VQA）基准，基于波兰医师和牙医专科认证考试真题构建，包含图像相关问题和纯文本QA对照集，并评估了多种视觉语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学VQA基准主要针对英语，缺乏对波兰语等高资源外语言的评估；同时医学领域的图像理解能力对AI临床应用至关重要，需要专门的基准来检验模型在多语言、医学视觉场景下的表现。

Method: 从波兰医学委员会认证考试中提取含图像的题目构建VQA基准，同时构建文本QA对照集；评估波兰语专用模型、通用开源模型和商业VLM；通过去掉图像、问题或两者的消融实验来评估视觉接地能力，并按图像重要性对问题分类。

Result: 最佳模型在完整VQA集上准确率为79.0%，仅GPT-5.6在可选答案的子集上超过人类参考水平；模型从问题文本中获取的信息比从图像中更多，在图像主导型问题上表现更差；所有模型在仅有答案选项时仍高于随机水平。

Conclusion: 波兰语医学VQA仍具较大挑战性，当前模型对图像的利用不足，过度依赖问题文本和答案选项的先验信息，这指出了多语言医学视觉语言模型未来改进的方向。

Abstract: We introduce a Polish-language medical visual question answering (VQA) benchmark, built from Polish Board Certification Examination questions for licensed physicians and dentists pursuing specialist certification. The benchmark comprises image-containing questions spanning diverse medical specialties and visual domains, together with a text-only question answering (QA) control set. We evaluate Polish-oriented, general-purpose open-weight, and commercial vision-language models. The task remains challenging: the best model achieves 79.0\% accuracy on the full VQA set, and only GPT-5.6 surpasses the approximate human reference on the subset with available candidate responses; all other evaluated models perform worse than humans. To assess visual grounding, we compare complete inputs with configurations omitting the image, the question, or both, and categorize questions by image importance. Models derive more useful information from the question text than from the image and perform worse on image-dominant questions. Across both QA and VQA, they nevertheless achieve above-chance accuracy from the answer choices alone, showing that non-trivial performance can persist even when key task components are missing.

</details>


### [207] [FlashDrive: Flash Vision-Language-Action Inference for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12932)
*Zekai Li,Yihao Liang,Hongfei Zhang,Jian Chen,Yesheng Liang,Zhijian Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlashDrive是一个面向视觉-语言-动作（VLA）自动驾驶模型的算法-系统协同设计框架，通过同时针对推理流水线中的四个瓶颈（视觉编码、语言模型预填充、推理token生成、流匹配去噪）提出轻量级算法捷径，并叠加CUDA Graph编译和内核融合等系统级优化，将端到端延迟从717ms降至151ms（4.7倍加速），在保持精度基本不变的情况下将10B参数模型推高到单GPU 6.6Hz，使端到端自动驾驶更接近实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽有望为自动驾驶带来端到端推理能力，但其计算成本过高无法满足实时控制需求。核心问题在于VLA推理不是单一瓶颈，而是四个级联的瓶颈：视觉编码在重叠视频帧上浪费算力、语言模型预填充重复计算上下文、推理token串行生成效率低、流匹配去噪对非均匀速度场使用均匀算力。单独解决任何一个阶段都无济于事，需要协同设计。

Method: FlashDrive提出算法-系统协同设计框架，同时针对四个瓶颈：1）利用时间重叠实现跨帧流式KV缓存重用；2）利用驾驶域推理的低熵和强块内相关性，采用非自回归扩散草稿模型用于投机解码；3）利用速度场"两端尖锐、中间平坦"的结构特性，实现自适应步长缓存；4）叠加系统级CUDA Graph编译和内核融合，使各技术相互叠加增益。模型使用W4A8量化。

Result: 应用于Alpamayo 1.5-10B模型，端到端延迟从717ms降至151ms（4.7倍加速），精度基本不变：minADE6@6.4s仅变化0.08m，minADE1有所提升，闭环碰撞率和偏离道路率在仿真中均有改善。推理频率从1.4Hz提升至6.6Hz（单GPU）。

Conclusion: 通过同时对VLA推理的四个级联瓶颈进行算法-系统协同优化，FlashDrive证明可以大幅降低端到端自动驾驶VLA模型的计算开销而不牺牲精度，使10B参数的推理型VLA模型接近实时部署要求。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models promise to bring end-to-end reasoning to autonomous driving, but their computational cost remains far too high for real-time control. The core challenge is structural: VLA inference is not a single bottleneck but a cascade of four. Visual encoding wastes compute on overlapping video frames; language-model prefill recomputes context that could be carried over from the previous timestep; reasoning tokens are generated serially despite low entropy; and flow-matching denoising applies uniform compute to a non-uniform velocity field. Addressing any one stage in isolation leaves the others untouched. We propose FlashDrive, an algorithm-system co-design framework that targets all four stages simultaneously. Our key insight is that each bottleneck admits a distinct, lightweight algorithmic shortcut: temporal overlap enables streaming KV-cache reuse across frames; the low per-token entropy and strong intra-block correlations of driving-domain reasoning make a non-autoregressive diffusion drafter highly effective for speculative decoding; and the velocity field's structure---sharp at the endpoints, flat in the middle---permits adaptive step caching that concentrates compute where it matters. Layered on system-level CUDA Graph compilation and kernel fusion, these techniques compound. Applied to Alpamayo 1.5-10B with W4A8 quantization, FlashDrive reduces end-to-end latency from 717ms to 151ms (4.7x) while leaving accuracy essentially unchanged: minADE6@6.4s shifts by only 0.08m, minADE1 improves, and closed-loop collision and off-road rates improve in simulation. By raising a 10B-parameter reasoning VLA from 1.4~Hz to 6.6~Hz on a single GPU, FlashDrive moves end-to-end autonomous driving substantially closer to real-time deployment.

</details>


### [208] [Decomposition of Evidence, Contradiction, and Fragility in Perturbation Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12935)
*Lei You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DECAF框架，将扰动解释中的模型响应分解为证据、矛盾和脆弱性三个组分，解决传统幅度解释无法区分响应含义的问题。在72模型ImageNet-9审计中，DECAF最大组分与独立行为的一致性达96.4%，远超幅度解释的35.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扰动方法仅测量预测变化的幅度，但相同幅度可能支持、反对最终事实-反事实差异，或在路径中出现而端点消失。需要一种能够解释响应含义而非仅测量大小的分解方法。

Method: DECAF通过逐步揭示配对输入来追踪对比发展轨迹，将对齐、对立和端点为零的响应分别路由为证据E、矛盾C和脆弱性F。分解严格保持幅度恒等关系Abs=E+C+F，并在端点相对公理下具有唯一性。

Result: 在受控视觉和表格设置中三个组分与独立测量行为一致。72模型审计中DECAF最大组分与观察行为一致性96.4%（幅度仅35.0%）。改变揭示路径使总响应增加近80%，但证据几乎不变而脆弱性增长4倍以上。在FunnyBirds和ImageNet-1k上优于通用归因基线；1B规模DINOv2上以4.75倍更低墙钟时间和2.36倍更低峰值内存匹配强梯度基线。

Conclusion: DECAF提供了一种比幅度更细粒度的扰动解释框架，能够区分响应方向、路径与端点特征，在解释准确性和计算效率上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Perturbation methods explain model decisions by measuring prediction changes under altered inputs, but response magnitude tells us only how much a model reacts, not what that reaction means. The same magnitude can support the final factual-counterfactual difference, oppose it, or arise strongly along the perturbation path yet vanish at the endpoint. We therefore track how the contrast develops as paired inputs are progressively revealed, using the final contrast to interpret the trajectory. We introduce DECAF (Decomposition of Evidence, Contradiction, And Fragility), which routes aligned, opposed, and endpoint-null responses into evidence E, contradiction C, and fragility F. The decomposition preserves ordinary magnitude exactly, Abs = E + C + F, and is unique under endpoint-relative axioms. Across controlled vision and tabular settings, the three components track independently measured behavior. In a 72-model ImageNet-9 audit, we compare cases with nearly identical response magnitude but different independently measured behaviors. The largest DECAF component agrees with an observed behavior in 96.4% of cases, compared with 35.0% for magnitude alone. Changing only the reveal path increases total response by nearly 80%, yet evidence barely changes while fragility grows by more than 4x. On FunnyBirds and ImageNet-1k, short forward-only DECAF trajectories outperform the tested general-purpose attribution baselines. On a 1B-scale DINOv2 model, a short trajectory matches a strong gradient-based baseline with 4.75x lower wall time and 2.36x lower peak memory.

</details>


### [209] [Moose: Latent concept learning with reasoning-shortcut awareness in $\mathcal{EL}^{++}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12961)
*Olga Mashkova,Asaad Mohammedsaleh,Fernando Zhapa-Camacho,Robert Hoehndorf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Moose方法，将OWL EL本体编译为可微分的Sentential Decision Diagram（SDD），用于神经符号学习中的潜在概念学习任务，并首次在OWL EL设置下进行推理捷径分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号学习方法只接受命题逻辑或Datalog，无法处理OWL 2 EL本体（如Gene Ontology和SNOMED CT等大型生产本体），且推理捷径问题在本体设置下尚未被研究。

Method: 将EL++ TBox和有限ABox编译为SDD作为可微分加权模型计数层，通过添加声明穷尽族的闭包子句来克服EL++在部分监督下表达能力受限的问题，并形式化定义了OWL EL本体上的部分监督潜在概念学习任务。

Result: 在MNIST-with-ontology和Pizzaïolo上评估，Moose优于命题神经符号方法、模糊逻辑和本体嵌入基线，并展示了首个OWL EL设置下的推理捷径分析。方法具有终止性、健全性、完备性和多项式中间规模，且在Lean中验证了证明。

Conclusion: Moose是首个在OWL EL本体上进行神经符号学习的方法，为大型生产本体的潜在概念学习提供了有效途径，并开创了该设置下推理捷径分析的研究方向。

Abstract: The OWL 2 EL profile is used in some of the largest production ontologies, including the Gene Ontology and SNOMED CT. Existing neuro-symbolic (NeSy) learning methods accept propositional theories or Datalog, and reasoning-shortcut (RS) awareness has not been investigated in ontology settings. We present Moose, a method that compiles an $\mathcal{EL}^{++}$ TBox and finite ABox to a Sentential Decision Diagram (SDD). The SDD acts as a differentiable weighted-model-counting layer, and we add closure clauses outside the $\mathcal{EL}^{++}$ profile on declared exhaustive families to overcome the limited expressivity of $\mathcal{EL}^{++}$ under partial supervision. We show termination, soundness, completeness, and polynomial intermediate sizes, and validate the proofs in Lean. We then define the first formal partial-supervision latent-concept-learning task over an OWL EL ontology, i.e., learning per-individual classifiers for latent concepts from observed ABox literals, and evaluate Moose on MNIST-with-ontology and Pizzaïolo. Moose improves over propositional-NeSy, fuzzy-logic, and ontology embedding baselines, and presents the first reasoning-shortcut analysis in an OWL EL setting.

</details>


### [210] [Foundations of MT-PDCL: Measure-Theoretic Probabilistic Definite Clause Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13018)
*Costin Bădică,Amelia Bădică*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MT-PDCL框架，将概率逻辑编程从有限离散领域推广到连续可测空间，通过Le Besgue积分定义声明性推理，替代离散grounding的组合瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统概率逻辑编程依赖将逻辑程序grounding为离散命题表示，这限制了精确推理只能处理有限领域和离散概率分布，无法原生支持连续可测空间上的推理。

Method: 引入测度论概率确定子句逻辑（MT-PDCL），在有界索引域上显式定义随机变量，用标准Borel σ-代数装备解释空间；基于连续分布语义建模概率规则；通过精确Lebesgue积分定义声明性蕴含；引入连续立即推论算子统一连续先验分布与连续观测的评估。

Result: 该方法将离散grounding的组合瓶颈替换为精确、代数化、结构可微的推理，在连续概率模型上获得表达能力，同时保留确定子句逻辑的纯声明性语法，代价是维度灾难的几何挑战。

Conclusion: MT-PDCL以测度论为基础，实现了逻辑变量在连续可测空间上的原生操作，弥合了概率逻辑编程与连续概率建模之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Standard probabilistic logic programming frameworks typically rely on grounding logic programs into discrete propositional representations. This operational requirement restricts exact inference to finite domains and discrete probability distributions. In this paper, we introduce Measure-Theoretic Probabilistic Definite Clause Logic (MT-PDCL), a generalized foundational framework that eliminates this finite-domain restriction. By explicitly defining stochastic variables over bounded index domains and equipping the interpretation space with standard Borel $σ$-algebras, MT-PDCL allows logical variables to operate natively over continuous measurable spaces. Building on Continuous Distribution Semantics, MT-PDCL models probabilistic rules as mutually independent causal events. However, rather than aggregating these derivations via finite boolean circuits, declarative entailment is formally defined through exact Lebesgue integration over the continuous measure space. We introduce a continuous immediate consequence operator that unifies the integration of continuous prior distributions with the evaluation of exact continuous observations. We demonstrate that this approach replaces the combinatorial bottleneck of discrete grounding with exact, algebraic, and structurally differentiable inference. While this transition trades discrete combinatorics for the geometric curse of dimensionality, it achieves the expressive power of continuous probabilistic models while preserving the pure declarative syntax of definite clause logic.

</details>


### [211] [From Local Mismatch to Global Impact: Optimizing Cache Reuse Policy for Efficient Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13043)
*Xichen Ye,Yifan Wu,Zhikang Xie,Xiangyu Yue,Cheng Jin,Weizhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Global-Impact Cache (GCache)，一种基于全局影响感知的缓存加速方法，通过理论推导误差传播上界并将其重新参数化为Bernstein形式，将缓存策略搜索建模为双层优化问题，以解决扩散模型缓存加速中局部相似性启发式与最终生成质量不对齐的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在视觉生成中表现优异但推理开销巨大。现有基于缓存的加速方法依赖局部相似性启发式，这种策略与最终生成质量之间存在显著偏差，因为误差在去噪轨迹上的传播和累积是不均匀的。

Method: 提出GCache框架：1) 建立误差传播上界的严格理论刻画；2) 鉴于该上界对复杂非凸扩散模型可能过于保守，用Bernstein形式对传播指数进行重新参数化；3) 将缓存策略搜索建模为双层优化问题——内层目标寻找最优重用策略，外层目标将误差加权函数与生成质量损失对齐。

Result: 在视频和图像生成任务上，GCache均优于已有缓存策略。在Wan2.1视频扩散模型上，保持2.17倍加速的同时显著提升生成质量，LPIPS从0.1095降至0.0316。

Conclusion: GCache通过将理论严谨性与经验性能相结合，能够学习优先将计算资源分配到对视觉保真度影响最大的位置，有效弥合了缓存策略与生成质量之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved dominant performance in visual generation but suffer from substantial inference overhead. While cache-based acceleration has emerged as a promising solution, existing policies rely on local similarity heuristics, which we identify as being significantly misaligned with final generation quality. This discrepancy stems from the non-uniform propagation and accumulation of errors along the denoising trajectory. To address this, we propose Global-Impact Cache (GCache). We first establish a rigorous theoretical characterization of the error propagation upper bound. Recognizing that this bound can be overly conservative for complex, highly non-convex diffusion models, we further reparameterize the propagation exponent with a Bernstein form and reformulate cache policy search as a bilevel optimization problem. In detail, GCache identifies an optimal reuse policy in the inner objective while aligning the error-weighting function with generation quality loss in the outer objective. This framework effectively reconciles theoretical rigor with empirical performance, learning to prioritize computation where it most impacts visual fidelity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GCache consistently outperforms prior caching strategies on both video and image generation. Notably, on the state-of-the-art Wan2.1 video diffusion model, GCache maintains a 2.17x speedup while significantly enhancing generation quality, reducing LPIPS from 0.1095 to 0.0316.

</details>


### [212] [BoardroomAI: Dependency-Aware Human-Steerable Multi-Agent Deliberation through Evolving Decision Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13046)
*Sanjeev Manivannan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BoardroomAI，一个将人类视为持续参与者而非一次性输入提供者的多智能体决策系统。通过类型化决策图、干预编译器、依赖感知传播和评估框架四个组件，实现人类在决策过程中随时挑战假设、修改约束、引入证据等干预操作。实验表明该方法在600个生成决策DAG干预中仅检查14.59%节点即匹配全面影响计算，并在12例试点中实现选择性修复，同时揭示"决策充分上下文闭合"的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于文本的多智能体系统中，人类只提供初始问题后便被动等待最终答案，无法在决策过程中进行干预。然而组织决策是一个持续演化的过程，需要人类在证据、约束和优先级变化时积极参与。作者旨在构建一个支持人机持续共存的决策系统。

Method: 系统包含四个核心组件：(i) 类型化决策图，建模证据、假设、约束、主张、反对意见、替代方案、风险、决策及语义依赖关系；(ii) 干预编译器，将人类确认的操作转换为显式图更新；(iii) 依赖感知传播机制，识别受影响的子图、保留未受影响工件并选择性重新激活相关专家；(iv) 评估框架，衡量干预影响、修复覆盖率、保留率、重计算量和决策有效性。

Result: 在600个生成决策DAG干预中，传播机制仅检查14.59%节点即匹配穷举影响计算结果。在12例试点中，选择性修复重计算62.11%的规范节点，完整保留所有金标准未受影响节点，6例产生有效更新决策，另外6例选择弃权。

Conclusion: 弃权案例表明，即使干预路由正确，系统仍可能缺乏足够的合成上下文来生成有效决策，这揭示了"决策充分上下文闭合"（decision-sufficient context closure）的必要性——即人类引导的多智能体审议需要确保决策所需上下文的完整聚合。所有结果为合成数据上的原型级验证。

Abstract: Organizational decisions are co-created while evidence, constraints, and human priorities continue to evolve. In conventional transcript-based multi-agent systems, humans typically provide an initial problem, agents deliberate internally, and the system returns a final response. BoardroomAI instead treats the human as a persistent participant who can intervene by challenging assumptions, modifying constraints, changing priorities, introducing evidence, or redirecting the decision process. We operationalize this human--agent coexistence through four components: (i) a typed decision graph representing evidence, assumptions, constraints, claims, objections, alternatives, risks, decisions, semantic dependencies, and specialist responsibility; (ii) an intervention compiler that converts confirmed human actions into explicit graph updates; (iii) dependency-aware propagation that identifies affected subgraphs, preserves unaffected artifacts, and selectively reactivates relevant specialists; and (iv) an evaluation framework measuring intervention impact, repair coverage, preservation, recomputation, and decision validity. Across 600 generated decision-DAG interventions, propagation matched exhaustive impact computation while inspecting only 14.59% of nodes. In a 12-case exploratory pilot, selective repair recomputed 62.11% of canonical nodes, preserved all gold-unaffected nodes, and produced valid updated decisions in six cases while abstaining in the remaining six. These abstentions show that correct intervention routing may still provide insufficient context for synthesis, motivating a \emph{decision-sufficient context closure} for human-steered multi-agent deliberation. All results are synthetic and prototype-level.

</details>


### [213] [DMDIntel: Interpreting Large Language Models via Dynamic Mode Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13048)
*Amogh Joshi,Animesh Mukherjee,Sergey Utyuzhnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DMDIntel是一种利用动态模式分解（DMD）技术来使LLM分类预测可解释的方法，通过分解隐藏状态并在多个数据集和模型上超越现有归因技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的预测缺乏可解释性，现有的归因方法（如PCA、集成梯度和SHAP）在性能上存在不足，需要更有效的输入归因技术来理解模型的决策过程。

Method: 提出DMDIntel方法，利用动态模式分解（DMD）将LLM的隐藏状态分解为显著模式（modes），然后基于这些模式上的投影值为输入token分配重要性排名，构建输入归因管道。

Result: 在三个数据集和三个模型家族上的严格实验表明，DMDIntel生成的token归因排名在性能上显著优于PCA、集成梯度和SHAP等最先进技术。

Conclusion: DMDIntel是一种有效的LLM预测解释方法，通过DMD分解实现更优的输入token归因，为大型语言模型的可解释性提供了新思路。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce DMDIntel which uses dynamic mode decomposition (DMD) to make the predictions made by LLMs in a classification task interpretable. It develops an input attribution pipeline, that first decomposes the hidden states of an LLM into prominent patterns, also known as modes, and then associates ranks to the input tokens based on the projection values on those modes. Rigorous experiments across three datasets and three model families consistently show that the ranked attribution of input tokens obtained using DMDIntel by far outperforms state-of-the-art techniques such as principal component analysis, integrated gradients and SHAP.

</details>


### [214] [VALG: An Agentic System for ML Theory Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13060)
*Dechen Zhang,Xuan Tang,Xinxiang Yin,Xingwu Chen,Jian Qian,Difan Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VALG，一个面向机器学习理论研究的自主智能体系统，通过多级验证、自适应问题表述和图结构证明开发相结合，在COLT 2026五个开放问题的九个子问题上进行评测，其中两个运行产生匹配源问题范围的定理候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习理论研究通常需要数学设定、问题表述和证明机制协同开发，这一过程是否可以被组织为自主智能体工作流尚不清楚。研究者希望探索这一过程的自动化可能性。

Method: VALG系统结合了三个核心组件：多级验证（Multi-level Verification）、自适应学习理论问题表述（Adaptive formulation）、图结构证明开发（Graph-structured proof development）。系统在每个定理分支中维护固定数学规范，检查类型化证明依赖图的定理级组合，并按依赖顺序构建和审查局部证明。当证明失败时，系统会分类障碍来源（推导、证明结构或定理表述）并相应调整路径。

Result: 在COLT 2026五个开放问题的九个子问题上的评测显示：两个运行产生了与源问题范围匹配的内部完成的定理候选；其余七个产生受限方法结果、特例或条件定理。案例研究展示了系统如何保持源范围匹配、松弛、条件结果和受阻尝试在数学上的区分。

Conclusion: VALG展示了机器学习理论研究过程可以被组织为自主智能体工作流，并且能够区分不同类型的数学结果状态，但仍有大量问题需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Machine learning theory studies learning procedures through mathematical setups in which the data model, training protocol, oracle access, loss, metric, and randomness define the phenomenon that a theorem is meant to explain. Solving an open problem therefore requires the problem formulation, theorem target, and proof mechanism to be developed in concert. Researchers formulate hypotheses, test them through preliminary theoretical or empirical analysis, and refine both assumptions and proofs. We investigate whether this process can be organized as an autonomous agentic workflow for ML theory research.
  We develop VALG, an agentic system that combines multi-level Verification, Adaptive formulation of Learning-theory problems, and Graph-structured proof development. Within each source-relative theorem branch, VALG maintains a fixed mathematical specification, checks the theorem-level composition of a typed proof-dependency graph, and constructs and reviews local proofs in dependency order. When a proof attempt fails, VALG identifies whether the obstruction lies in a derivation, the proof structure, or the theorem formulation and routes the next attempt accordingly. Formulation-level obstructions initiate an explicitly related variant or relaxation, preserving the mathematical relation between the resulting theorem and the source problem.
  We evaluate VALG on nine subproblems from five COLT 2026 open problems. Two runs produce internally finalized theorem candidates that match the scope of their source briefs; the remaining seven yield restricted-method results, special cases, or conditional theorems. These case studies show how VALG keeps source-scope matches, relaxations, conditional results, and blocked attempts mathematically distinct.
